Use this skill whenever the user asks to implement a slice, add a feature, or generate code from the event model. This is the v2 of evdb-dev — same output, less AI work. - **evdb-diff**: Audits the entire `.eventmodel/.slices/index.json` against the actual `src/BusinessCapabilities/` codebase to synchronize slice statuses. Updates each slice to "Planned" if no implementation is found, or "Review" if code exists (never downgrades a "Done" slice). Use this skill whenever the user wants to know what changed in the event model, what needs to be implemented or deleted, what slices are new or missing, asks for a diff/delta/action list from the event model index, or asks to sync/update slice statuses. Trigger even if the user just says "what changed?", "what do I need to implement?"… ([outsidenote/evdb-blueprint](https://github.com/outsidenote/evdb-blueprint/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/evdb-diff))
- **evdb-normalize**: Extracts all deterministic structured data from slice.json files into .normalized.json intermediate files (Option C normalization). Handles naming conversions, type mapping, field classification, spec GWT scenarios, and view state field derivation. Predicate boolean expressions are left as null with review_required: true — no guessing. Use before evdb-scaffold to provide it a richer, pre-parsed input. ([outsidenote/evdb-blueprint](https://github.com/outsidenote/evdb-blueprint/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/evdb-normalize))
- **evdb-scaffold**: Deterministic scaffold generator for evdb slices. Reads slice.json definitions from .eventmodel/ and generates all TypeScript boilerplate files (events, views, command, gwts, adapter, endpoint, tests) without AI. The command handler body is left as a TODO for the AI to fill in. Use this skill for deterministic testing of evdb-dev output, or to pre-generate boilerplate before AI fills in business logic. Trigger for phrases like "scaffold the slice", "generate boilerplate", "pre-generate the files". ([outsidenote/evdb-blueprint](https://github.com/outsidenote/evdb-blueprint/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/evdb-scaffold))
- **evdb-test**: Runs regression tests for evdb-diff and evdb-dev skills using fixture-based event model states. Use this skill when the user asks to test the evdb skills, run fixtures, validate skill behavior, or check for regressions. Trigger for phrases like "test the diff skill", "test evdb-dev", "run the fixtures", "check for regressions", or "validate evdb against test data". ([outsidenote/evdb-blueprint](https://github.com/outsidenote/evdb-blueprint/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/evdb-test))
- **evdb-verify**: Contract verifier for evdb slices. Compares generated TypeScript files against .normalized.json spec to detect drift, missing implementations, and structural violations. Marks each finding confirmed or refuted. Use when asked to test accessibility at runtime, confirm findings in the simulator or check that repairs held. For a static audit use evenbetter-analyze. ([MelloBirkan/EvenBetterFramework](https://github.com/MelloBirkan/EvenBetterFramework/tree/HEAD/plugins/evenbetter-ios/skills/evenbetter-accessibility-tests) · ★1)
- **evenbetter-analyze**: Audits a Swift or SwiftUI repository against the EvenBetter corpus of Apple HIG and accessibility clauses, checks each finding on Apple's docs, and writes an HTML report with evidence and fixes. Use when asked to analyze, audit, scan or review an iOS app for HIG conformance, VoiceOver, Dynamic Type, contrast, tap targets or WCAG. To re-check an existing report, use evenbetter-validate. ([MelloBirkan/EvenBetterFramework](https://github.com/MelloBirkan/EvenBetterFramework/tree/HEAD/plugins/evenbetter-ios/skills/evenbetter-analyze) · ★1)
- **evenbetter-app-launcher**: Guides a chosen iOS app through Xcode setup, pricing, paywalls and trials, screenshots and metadata, App Review and rejections, marketing, updates and analytics. Use when the user asks how to price, submit, get approved, market or update an iOS app, why it was rejected, or what comes next after launch. To check whether an idea has a market, use evenbetter-app-opportunity-research. ([MelloBirkan/EvenBetterFramework](https://github.com/MelloBirkan/EvenBetterFramework/tree/HEAD/plugins/evenbetter-ios/skills/evenbetter-app-launcher) · ★1)
- **evenbetter-app-opportunity-research**: Researches an iOS App Store niche on the web, with a cited URL for every claim, and produces a competitor, revenue and complaint map, a feature gap matrix, three ranked concepts and an MVP brief. Use when the user asks what app to build or whether an idea has demand, or wants research on App Store gaps, a category or competitors. To ship or market a chosen app, use evenbetter-app-launcher. ([MelloBirkan/EvenBetterFramework](https://github.com/MelloBirkan/EvenBetterFramework/tree/HEAD/plugins/evenbetter-ios/skills/evenbetter-app-opportunity-research) · ★1)
- **evenbetter-design**: Gives SwiftUI screens a deliberate visual identity. It grounds the design in the subject, drafts a plan for review (palette, type roles, wireframe), then builds it within Apple HIG and accessibility rules. Use when asked to style or restyle an iOS screen or app, pick a palette or typography, or avoid a template look. For component structure use evenbetter-swiftui-ui-patterns. ([MelloBirkan/EvenBetterFramework](https://github.com/MelloBirkan/EvenBetterFramework/tree/HEAD/plugins/evenbetter-ios/skills/evenbetter-design) · ★1)
- **evenbetter-general-epic**: Runs a staged, product-first workflow for large work on any stack: clarifying questions, an epic brief, HTML core flows and a tech plan, each validated, then tickets, execution and review. Use when the user wants to plan or build a new product area or a change that spans several flows or teams. For one scoped feature use evenbetter-general-feature; for iOS use evenbetter-ios-epic. ([MelloBirkan/EvenBetterFramework](https://github.com/MelloBirkan/EvenBetterFramework/tree/HEAD/plugins/evenbetter-general/skills/evenbetter-general-epic) · ★1)
- **evenbetter-general-feature**: Runs a staged, question-driven workflow for one scoped feature on any stack, such as an API, web app or CLI: clarifying questions, a plan and its stress test, tickets, execution and a review against the plan. Use when the user asks to plan a change together before any code is written, or to ticket, build or review one feature, refactor or bug fix. For multi-flow product work use evenbetter-general-epic; for iOS use evenbetter-ios-feature. ([MelloBirkan/EvenBetterFramework](https://github.com/MelloBirkan/EvenBetterFramework/tree/HEAD/plugins/evenbetter-general/skills/evenbetter-general-feature) · ★1)
- **evenbetter-ios-app-intents**: Designs App Intents, app entities and App Shortcuts that expose an iOS app's actions and content to Shortcuts, Siri, Spotlight, widgets and controls, including iOS 27 schemas and snippets. Use when adding AppIntent, AppEntity or EntityQuery types or Shortcuts phrases, or when routing an intent into the app. To plan the screen an intent opens, use evenbetter-ios-feature. ([MelloBirkan/EvenBetterFramework](https://github.com/MelloBirkan/EvenBetterFramework/tree/HEAD/plugins/evenbetter-ios/skills/evenbetter-ios-app-intents) · ★1)
- **evenbetter-ios-debugger-agent**: Builds the current iOS project, runs it on a simulator, drives its UI and reads screenshots, logs and debugger state to explain runtime behavior, through Xcode's MCP server, MobileBuildMCP or xcodebuild. Use when asked to run the app, tap through a flow, take a simulator screenshot, read console output or reproduce a crash. For accessibility or HIG audits, use evenbetter-analyze. - **evenbetter-ios-epic**: Plans and reviews multi-screen iOS work, such as a complex flow or a whole new app, in closed-question stages: UX brief, HTML screen flows, HIG tech plan, tickets, execution and review. Use when designing navigation across several screens, planning an app from scratch, or reviewing a built multi-screen flow against its specs. For one screen or a small change use evenbetter-ios-feature. Use when adding, reviewing or refactoring haptics, or when a haptic does not play. For a view's wider accessibility review, use evenbetter-swiftui-accessibility. To re-check findings without editing code, use evenbetter-validate. Use when asked to validate, verify or double-check an EvenBetter report. To scan for new issues, use evenbetter-analyze.
